Runs at startup (CPU-heavy), never on the save hot path. Plus backup **dedup**: a save whose prior file == the newest backup skips both backup writes. **(5) Recovery-chain walk** — `_safe_load_json`, after main + legacy `.bak` both fail, walks the timestamped rotation newest→oldest (decompressing `.gz`), restoring from the first that parses (renames corrupt main to `*.corrupt-<ts>` first). **(6) Temp read-back validation** — before `os.replace`, the freshly-fsynced temp is re-read: full `json.loads` + entry-count for ≤ `_SAVE_READBACK_FULL_PARSE_MAX_BYTES` (32 MB), cheap tail check (`}`) above; mismatch raises so the live file is left intact. **(7) Startup housekeeping** — `_run_data_dir_housekeeping` (daemon thread off the UI) compresses + byte-caps backups + prunes `lost_entries/`, reclaiming EXISTING residue (observed: 6.9 GB → ~1 GB). **SACRED: don't weaken the shrink guard for the non-mirror path — it caught the 2026-05-22 nuke twice.** |
| `[INV-100]` | **Seamless-cure verification — BOTH Scrub routes.** Before primers are shown as orderable, each route PROVES the lab product equals the cured plasmid byte-for-byte: QuikChange via `_scrub_qc_verify` (simulate the primers on the ORIGINAL template — the forward footprint + the top-strand bases of the reverse footprint overwrite `orig`; the result MUST equal `cured`, so a cure outside every primer's reach is caught), Golden Braid via `_scrub_gb_verify` (digest+ligate — see [INV-99]). Surfaced as a "✓ Verified" line in the report, a `verified` flag on the plan, and the `scrub-plasmid` endpoint. `_scrub_design` independently guarantees `cured` is substitution-only and differs from `orig` ONLY at edits that each destroy a target site — so "verified" means the primers reproduce a plasmid whose only changes are the destroyed cut sites. Don't drop either verifier or gate it behind a flag: it is the catastrophic-class safety net for primer-driven curing. |
| `[INV-99]` | **Scrub Golden Braid** — `_scrub_gb_design` / `_scrub_gb_*` helpers, MutagenizeModal Scrub-tab "Re-circularize: Golden Braid" mode, `scrub-plasmid` agent `method:"golden_braid"`. Alternative to QuikChange: split the plasmid at each cure cluster into PCR fragments whose primers carry a BsaI tail (`GCGC` + `GGTCTC` + `A` + native binding); a Golden Gate (BsaI) reaction reassembles them. The 4 nt junction overhang IS the native binding's first 4 nt, sliced from the CURED template (binding == display, catastrophic-class), so reassembly is seamless EXCEPT the cures. **BsaI is the assembly enzyme → it is force-added to the cure set**; EVERY BsaI site must be cured (an internal one is cut mid-reaction). A BsaI site that lands in `sites_skipped` (uncurable, e.g. an opposite-strand CDS with no synonym) FAILS the whole design — you cannot Golden-Gate around a site the enzyme still cuts — the "an unwanted site IS BsaI" edge case. Junctions sit at cure clusters (tighter footprint than QuikChange so both primer arms reach every cure); each junction's overhang must pass `_scrub_gb_overhang_ok` (4 ACGT, non-palindromic, not a homopolymer) and be unique vs all others + their RCs, sliding ±10 bp to find one. Catastrophic-class `_scrub_gb_verify`: rebuilds each amplicon as REAL PCR produces it (cure-bearing primer ends + ORIGINAL-template middle — a cure outside primer reach would NOT make it in), asserts exactly 2 BsaI sites per amplicon, ligates by chaining native overhangs, and the reassembled circle MUST equal the cured plasmid AND be BsaI-CLEAN (the assembly enzyme — a residual BsaI re-cuts the product). A NON-assembly unwanted site that couldn't be silently cured stays in `sites_skipped` and leaves the product unchanged there — like QuikChange, that is reported, NOT a design failure (the verify scans only for the assembly enzyme, not the full forbidden set). A lone cure cluster → a single fragment that self-circularises (one non-palindromic overhang). Reuses `_scrub_design` (cure engine), `_pick_binding_region` (Tm), `_circ_extract`, `_rc`. |

| `[INV-70]` | Bulk-align matcher: sequence-first ranking + memory cap (2026-05-24). Pre-fix the matcher ran name-substring first and only fell back to k-mer when name match failed — a coincidental substring (e.g. `smp2` ⊂ `pcambia1300`) could outrank a 99%-identical entry by sequence. Post-fix `_match_samples_to_library` always computes both name and k-mer scores for every (sample, library entry) pair; decision tree: **strong-kmer (Jaccard ≥ 0.5)** wins outright, then **near-exact name (≥ 0.85)** as fallback, then **weak-kmer (≥ 0.2)** as last resort, else propose add-as-new. Top-3 alternatives surfaced on every match for user visibility. `_kmer_set(seq, cap=50_000)` truncates sequences > 50 kb to bound memory — a 154 kb chloroplast genome × 89 library entries exploded RAM to >5 GB pre-fix. `BulkAlignConfirmModal` now shows k-mer and name as separate colour-coded columns (green ≥ 50%, yellow ≥ 20%, red below) so weak matches stand out. |
| `[INV-71]` | Strand-agnostic alignment (canonical k-mers + RC trials) (2026-05-24). Pre-fix a Plasmidsaurus consensus that assembled in the opposite orientation to its library entry scored 0% k-mer overlap in `_match_samples_to_library` and ~0% identity in `_pick_best_rotation` — the matcher then picked a coincidental backbone match and the aligner produced garbage. Post-fix `_kmer_set(canonical=True)` (default) normalises each k-mer to the lexicographically smaller of (k-mer, RC(k-mer)) so RC-of-target samples now score 100% Jaccard. `_pick_best_rotation` tries forward AND RC of the query at the plain tier, then runs rotation candidates in BOTH orientations when neither plain crosses the 80% threshold (4 rotation trials max). Result carries new `query_rc: bool` field. Early-stop at `_ROTATION_EARLY_STOP_PCT = 99.5` avoids wasting C-loop time once a near-perfect candidate lands. Verified end-to-end on user case: Smp-2/3/5/6/7 → DEMO 39/38/41/42/43 at 100% k-mer / 100% identity (pre-fix: all → pCAMBIA1300 at 40% / 47%). |
| `[INV-72]` | Alignment audit sweep (2026-05-25). Five-part hardening that closes gaps the `[INV-67]`–`[INV-71]` sweeps left behind. **(1) `_normalize_dna_for_align` helper**: every `_pairwise_align` input runs through a single normaliser that strips FASTA header lines (`>name\n` via `_FASTA_HEADER_PATTERN`, line-anchored), digits, whitespace, then uppercases + IUPAC-validates against `_IUPAC_NUC_CHARS = frozenset("ACGTUMRWSYKVHDBN")`. Pre-sweep the docstring claimed "raises on non-IUPAC" but no such check existed — a pasted protein sequence or FASTA-as-is body either crashed deep inside Biopython or produced silent garbage. `_pick_best_rotation` pre-normalises at entry so its frame-shift helpers (`_rotate_aligned_to_original_target_frame` / `_rotate_aligned_to_original_query_frame`) use the cleaned length — pre-fix a raw-FASTA input would mean `len(target_seq)` in the shift differed from what `_pairwise_align` consumed, off-by-N depending on how many header/whitespace chars got scrubbed. **(2) Agent endpoint picker parity**: `_h_diff_plasmid` and `_h_align_plasmidsaurus_zip` now route through `_pick_best_rotation` (canvas_axis="query" and "target" respectively) instead of a bare `_find_circular_alignment_offset` + `_pairwise_align`. Pre-sweep agent callers missed the RC-orientation auto-detection + multi-rotation best-of-N pick the UI gained in `[INV-71]` — an RC-orientation Plasmidsaurus consensus scored 0% via the agent while the UI scored 100%. Response payload gains `picked_rotation` / `query_rotation` / `target_rotation` / `query_rc` fields so the agent can replicate the alignment; `rotation_offset` kept as a back-compat alias for `target_rotation`. **(3) Bulk-align failure surfacing**: `_bulk_align_worker`'s add-as-new path tracks success vs failure in a shared `add_counters = {"ok", "err"}` dict; the OSError/ValueError/RuntimeError catch increments `counters["err"]` rather than letting the count of "tried to add" pass for "actually saved". Summary toast now shows `aligned N · added M new · add failed K · failed L` with severity bumped to "warning" when any failure column is non-zero. Mid-flight cancel readout reads from the same counter so it agrees with the final summary. **(4) `n_failed` log diagnostic**: the no-gbk-member skip in bulk-align now logs `BulkAlign: skipping sample %r — no .gbk member field` so a malformed Plasmidsaurus manifest leaves a trail in the diagnostic bundle (pre-fix the skip was silent and "failed N" gave no clue why). **(5) Coverage clamp**: `_align_worker`'s toast and `VerificationReportModal._collect_rows` clamp `coverage_pct` to `min(100.0, …)` so a corrupted result dict can't render ">100% coverage" to the user. Underlying numbers in storage are untouched — display-only defense. Tests: `TestNormalizeDnaForAlign` (12 cases), `TestPickBestRotationNormalises` (3), `TestAgentDiffPlasmidUsesPicker` (2), `TestAgentAlignPlasmidsaurusUsesPicker` (1), `TestPairwiseAlignCoverageClampInToast` (3), `TestBulkAlignNoGbkLogs` (1). |
| `[INV-73]` | Alignment + F9 diagnostic follow-up sweep (2026-05-25). Closes gaps the `[INV-72]` sweep left behind after a parallel-agent audit. **Alignment (5):** (1) **`_flush_active_alignments` thread-safety** — the load-merge-save sequence now holds `_cache_lock` for the entire RMW. `_diff_align_worker`, `_align_worker` (Plasmidsaurus), `_multi_align_worker` run in different `@work` groups, so two concurrent flushes against the same library entry would clobber each other's writes (silent data-loss path). RLock allows the inner `_load_library`/`_save_library` to re-enter without deadlock. (2) **`_extract_variants_from_alignment` result cap** — `_MAX_VARIANTS_PER_ALIGNMENT = 10_000` bounds memory on a fully-divergent alignment (200 kb random vs 200 kb random ≈ 200k variant dicts ≈ 40 MB). Capped runs emit a `{"type":"truncated"}` sentinel so callers can surface "10k+" rather than silently underreport; type-filter callers (`sum(1 for v in variants if v["type"]=="snp")`) naturally skip it. (3) **`_kmer_set` minimum for strong-match** — `_MIN_KMER_SET_FOR_STRONG_MATCH = 50` gates the `kmer-strong` path in `_match_samples_to_library` so a 25 bp sample (~6 canonical k-mers) can no longer score a coincidental 1.0 Jaccard against a library entry that happens to contain a primer-length match region. Below the threshold the sample falls through to name / weak-kmer / add-as-new. (4) **Rotation-picker enum validation** — `_deserialize_stored_alignment_args` validates `picked_rotation` ∈ {none, query, target}, `query_rotation`/`target_rotation` non-negative int, `query_rc` bool; corrupted values coerce to safe defaults with a warning log rather than crashing or silently misframing downstream segments. (5) **`_coverage_pct_from_result` helper** — centralises the clamp + zero-target guard so toast (`_align_worker`) and `VerificationReportModal._collect_rows` can't drift in their display logic; future call sites get the same defensive math. **F9 / diagnostic bundle (2):** (6) **`events_summary.json`** in the diagnostic bundle — `_extract_recent_events` parses the last 200 structured `_log_event` lines out of the rotating log (walks rotated backups too) and ships them as a JSON-parseable array; a bug-reader (or an AI scanning the bundle) gets the causal chain without regex against unstructured text. (7) **Terminal/locale env capture** in `_build_system_info` — narrow allowlist (`TERM`, `COLORTERM`, `TERM_PROGRAM`, `LANG`, `LC_*`, `SHELL`, `WSL_DISTRO_NAME`, `XDG_SESSION_TYPE`) + TTY flags so rendering bugs on `TERM=dumb` / `LANG=C` / non-TTY pipes are visible in triage. **Event logger (3):** (8) `_bulk_align_worker` emits `alignment.bulk.summary` event (n_aligned/n_added/n_add_failed/n_failed/n_committed) so the diagnostic bundle captures the bulk-align outcome even after the toast is dismissed. (9) `_align_worker` and `_diff_align_worker` exception logs now name query/target ids + lengths + topology — pre-fix "alignment failed" bug reports lacked enough context to reproduce. (10) `_action_log` decorator's silent-except now leaves a `_log.debug` breadcrumb when `_log_event` itself fails — pre-fix a malformed payload silently swallowed the entry with no trace. **Pyright cleanup:** `pyrightconfig.json` adds `extraPaths: ["."]` (lets tests resolve `import splicecraft`), suppresses `reportUnreachable` + `reportUnnecessaryComparison` globally (defensive code at module + API boundaries is intentional), and adds an `executionEnvironments` block for `tests/` that suppresses Textual-Widget generic-type narrowing issues (`reportAttributeAccessIssue`, `reportOptionalMemberAccess`, `reportArgumentType`, …) — keeps main source strict while documenting tests' looser typing as intentional. Real bugs fixed in pass: `test_smoke.py::_timed` unbound `result`, duplicate method name in `test_genbank_io.py`. Workspace pyright now reports `0 errors`. Tests: `TestVariantExtractionCap` (4), `TestExtractVariantsMixedAndDivergent` (2), `TestPickedRotationEnumValidation` (6), `TestCoveragePctHelper` (6), `TestKmerSetForStrongMatchThreshold` (2), `TestAlignmentQualityStatusBoundaries` (5), `TestFlushAlignmentsLocked` (2). Existing test `TestMatchSamplesToLibrary::test_sequence_match_beats_name_substring` updated to use a non-repetitive 1500 bp seed (was 27 bp tandem repeat, below the new strong-match k-mer threshold). `test_sweep22.py::test_every_load_helper_uses_safe_load_json` updated to walk one level of call-graph indirection (`_load_library` now routes through `_ensure_library_cache_populated_and_migrated` post-INV-72 cache rewrite). |
| `[INV-88]` | Bulk-align confirm window: real per-sample alignment quality, aligned ONCE (2026-05-31). Follow-up to `[INV-87]` after the user noted the BulkAlignConfirmModal's "k-mer" column reads "100%" for a read with a known 1-bp mismatch. Cause is conceptual + cosmetic: the k-mer column is a **matching** score (canonical-k-mer Jaccard — "which library entry is this read?"), not alignment identity, and for a circular plasmid the origin-junction k-mers alone drop a *perfect* read to ~99.8% Jaccard (measured on JP4W9V: DEMO40-1/2/3 perfect reads = 99.78% Jaccard; DEMO40-4 with one SNP = 99.55%) — all of which the cell's `f"{kmer:.0%}"` then rounds to "100%". Massaging the k-mer would mislead the *other* way (perfect reads → "99.8%"). Fix surfaces the TRUE quality: **Ident / Mism / Gaps** columns showing each read's real `_pick_best_rotation(..., is_circular=<target topology>, mode="global", canvas_axis="target")` against its matched plasmid. **Aligned exactly once** — `_compute_bulk_quality` runs on the `_match_samples_worker` button-press thread (right after `_match_samples_to_library`, progress ticks the bar), aligns each target-bearing match, and stashes BOTH a display summary (`_aln = {ident, mism, gaps}`) and the full result (`_aln_result`) on the match dict; `BulkAlignConfirmModal` then only RENDERS `_aln` (no in-modal worker), and `_bulk_align_worker` REUSES `_aln_result` (verbatim if it carries `aligned_q`/`aligned_t`, else falls back to a fresh align) so the commit doesn't re-run the ~18 kb pairwise. **The first build of this feature aligned the same read THREE times** (modal-open preview worker `_quality_align_worker` + commit) — the modal "hung" re-aligning on open; the user flagged the double work, so it was reworked to align-once-up-front before shipping (the in-modal `_quality_align_worker`/`_apply_quality_row` + the modal's `zip_path` param were removed). Display routes through the pure `_bulk_quality_cells(aln, *, action, has_target)`: em-dash for non-`align` / no-target rows, "…" while pending (unreached now — `_aln` is always set before the modal opens), "?" on align failure, else honest `_format_identity_pct` (never a false "100%") coloured by `_identity_pct_color` + yellow mismatch / red gap counts. `_aln` is stripped from the confirm payload (`action_run`); `_aln_result` rides through for the commit's reuse. Verified read-only on JP4W9V: CAM 4 (DEMO40-4) renders `99.99% · 1 · 0`, the three perfect reads `100% · 0 · 0`. Tests: `TestBulkQualityCells` (8), `TestBulkAlignConfirmModalQualityColumns` (2). |
| `[INV-95]` | Ctrl+E `EditSeqDialog` rework (2026-06-01, user request). The sweep-#39 modal kept its hardened bones (RadioSet + `_InstantPressButton` OK/Cancel, one-shot `dismiss` guard, `_sanitize_pasted_sequence`, IUPAC validation, `_MAX_INPUT_BP = 200_000` cap) — the user explicitly chose to KEEP the radios over a button rewrite ("keep the radios if those are more hardened"). Changes: **(1) Insert split into Insert LEFT / Insert RIGHT.** New internal modes `insert_left` / `insert_right`; `_MODE_INSERT = "insert"` retained as a back-compat alias `__init__` maps to insert_left (old callers + `test_modal_boundaries` pass `"insert"`). The DISMISS payload still speaks the App's language — insert_left → `(bases, "insert", start, start)`, insert_right → `(bases, "insert", end, end)` — so `_edit_dialog_result` + `_rebuild_record_with_edit` (feature-coord shift, sacred invariant #9) are unchanged; insert-right is just an insert at the region END, which the existing `_shift_range` handles. **(2) Colour-coded live warning** (`_build_preview_line`): "You are about to `[b <color>]<action>[/]` `[b <color>]<N> bp[/]` `[<color>]<position>[/]`" — green insert / red delete / yellow replace; position is "to the left of cursor" / "to the right of cursor" / "at cursor"; `<N>` is the TEXTBOX count for insert/replace (live as the user types) and the REGION count for delete. Rebuilt on every mode-change + `TextArea.Changed`. **(3) Region-prefilled textbox, always visible.** `on_mount` seeds the box with the region's bases; `_apply_mode_visibility` no longer HIDES the box for delete — instead it sets `TextArea.read_only=True` so it shows a read-only preview of the affected bases (label: "Bases that will be deleted (read-only preview):"). `_on_mode_changed` manages content: delete → show region; insert → clear an untouched prefill (or a preview carried over from delete) so it starts blank; replace → seed with the region when empty / arriving from a preview. **(4) Cursor = 1-bp region.** `action_edit_seq` opens a bare cursor on `[pos, min(pos+1, n))` (was an empty `[pos, pos)` insert point) — a single base edits like a selection; past the last base it falls back to a bare insert point. **`_cursor_pos` is the EXACT base under the cursor, no off-by-one:** a plain click sets `_cursor_pos = bp` (the clicked base; the shift-select path uses `e = max(anchor, bp) + 1`, half-open) and the renderer reverse-videos the same base (`is_cur = (cursor_pos == i)` in `_build_seq_text`), so `seq[pos:pos+1]` is precisely what the user sees highlighted — locked by a boundary-covering test. **(5) Ctrl+E default is NON-destructive; Delete key routes to Delete.** `action_edit_seq` (Ctrl+E) opens **Replace** for a selection / **Insert-left** for a bare cursor — NEVER Delete (user: "ctrl+e SHOULD NOT DEFAULT TO DELETE!!! huge hand slip risk"). The shared opener `_open_seq_edit_dialog(prefer=…)` takes `"context"` (Ctrl+E) or `"delete"`. `action_delete_feature` (the **Delete key**): with a feature selected it deletes the feature as before; with NO feature it calls `_open_seq_edit_dialog(prefer="delete")` → this dialog in Delete mode with the cursor bp / selection prefilled + Delete radio pre-selected + OK focused (a modal confirm — never an instant delete; undoable via `_edit_dialog_result`'s `_push_undo`), replacing the old "No feature selected" notify. Insert modes open with an EMPTY box (`on_mount` — a prefilled region would insert a duplicate). **Hardening — `_PREFILL_MAX_BP = 20_000`:** a region larger than the cap opens with an EMPTY box. Critical for Replace (whose submit READS the textbox) — a truncated prefill would silently commit truncated bases; and it avoids rendering a whole plasmid into the `TextArea`. Delete acts on the region (`[s,e]`), never the textbox, so nothing is lost; the label reads "(too large to preview — see the count above)". **Declutter (user: "ui crowded and lil messy"):** five horizontal radios overflowed the 66-col dialog content — **Copy was dropped** (redundant with the seq panel's Ctrl+C, and the user's original ask was four ops) and the redundant "Mode:" label removed, leaving four radios (64 cols) that fit; `min-width: 76` on `#edit-dlg` overrides a ~72-col render cap for ~6 cols of spare. **Radio highlight sync:** Textual's `RadioSet._on_mount` runs `action_next_button` → highlights index 0 regardless of which radio is `value=True`, so a non-first default (Delete via the Delete key, Replace for a selection) showed the `-selected` highlight box on Insert-left while a different radio was filled (user: "the delete radio is selected but the first radio is highlighted"). `on_mount` re-syncs via `rs._selected = rs.pressed_index` (deferred with `call_after_refresh`, guarded — private attr). **Post-edit highlight + scroll-into-view (2026-06-01):** on submit, `_edit_dialog_result` highlights the modified region (inserted / replaced bases, expanded to the new length) and centers it via `focus_span(s, s+len(new_bases), center=True, select=True)`; a pure deletion (`len == 0`) just parks the cursor at the splice point. **Critical interaction:** the async restriction-scan re-paint (`_dispatch_restr_scan` → `_apply`, `call_from_thread`) calls `update_seq`, which resets cursor + selection — it would WIPE the just-set edit highlight a beat later. `_apply` now snapshots + restores `_cursor_pos` / `_user_sel` / `_sel_range` / `_sel_anchor` across the overlay re-render (the sequence is unchanged — only the restriction overlay faded in). This also fixes a LATENT bug: every scan completion (on load, `r`-toggle, edit) used to silently clear the user's cursor + selection. Tests: `test_smoke.py::TestEditSeqDialog` (30 — alias→insert_left, insert_right preserved, left/right dispatch positions, warning action/count/position/colour, prefill + read-only, Ctrl+E selection → **replace** (not delete), Ctrl+E cursor → **insert-left** empty box, Delete-key-no-feature → Delete mode, **cursor-bp read is exact across boundaries (no off-by-one)**, **four radios fit within the dialog (no overflow)**, **radio highlight matches the checked mode**, **post-edit insert/replace highlights the modified region + delete parks the cursor**, huge-region empty box, plus the retained sanitize / delete-as-replace-empty / idempotent-dismiss cases). Help table + CHANGELOG updated. |

App handler `_map_alignment_lane_clicked` drives the seq panel. Closes the "No public jump-to-bp helper" gap flagged in `_jump_to_library_entry_at_pos` (verification-report jump refactored onto it). ALL coords ABSOLUTE; the panel converts at render via `_abs_to_disp` (so a non-zero rotation still lands the right base — catastrophic-class for an editor people use to fix exact bp). **(C) Ctrl+F fuzzy sequence search.** `_search_subsequence(seq, query, *, max_mismatches, circular, both_strands, max_hits=_SEQ_SEARCH_MAX_HITS=10_000)` — Hamming (substitutions only, NO indels), IUPAC-aware via `_iupac_compatible`, both inputs scrubbed/validated through `_normalize_dna_for_align` (U→T, FASTA/whitespace/digit strip, `ValueError` on a foreign char). FORWARD (top-strand) coords for BOTH strands — a `-` hit means RC(query) sits on the top strand there (query anneals to the bottom strand), the same convention as sacred invariant #2's bottom-strand cuts. Circular scans `seq + seq[:m-1]` (a wrap hit's `end` may exceed `n`; `start` always in `[0,n)`); a palindromic query (`rc == q`) is skipped on the reverse pass and any same-span `-`/`+` pair de-dups to `+` so `n`/`N` never visits a bp twice. `SeqSearchModal` collects query + mismatch budget + both-strands, computes hits ONCE on submit, and dismisses with `{query, mismatches, both_strands, hits}` (a 0-hit / bad-char search STAYS OPEN with a status hint rather than dismissing). App: `action_find_sequence` (Ctrl+F) → `_on_seq_search` (stores `_seq_search`, jumps to the first hit at/after the cursor, wrapping) → `_goto_search_hit` (focus_span center+select + toast `idx+1/N · bp · strand · n/N`); `action_find_next`/`action_find_prev` step `n`/`N` with wrap. Each hit lands as a `_user_sel` selection so **Alt+Shift+F** annotates it on the spot (the find-a-repeat → annotate → find-next workflow the user described). **(D) Keybinding remap.** Ctrl+F `add_feature` → **`find_sequence`**; `add_feature` → **Alt+Shift+F** (on BOTH `PlasmidApp` and `SynthesisScreen`; the SynthesisEditor/ProteinEditor key-forwarders gained `alt+shift+f`). **NOT Alt+F** — Textual decodes the Alt+F byte sequence (`ESC f`, readline forward-word) as `ctrl+right`, so an `alt+f` binding never fires and the keystroke just nudges the seq cursor 1 bp right (user bug report 2026-06-01: "alt+f seems to just offset highlighted bases by 1bp to the right"); `ESC F` (Shift) decodes cleanly as `alt+shift+f`. Same `ESC`-prefix trap silently broke the latent `alt+b` BLAST-menu shortcut (`ESC b` → `ctrl+left`); that dead binding was REMOVED (user request) — BLAST is Ctrl+B (modal) + menu-bar click only. The File menu's `alt+f` was retired for the SAME reason (undeliverable, not because add-feature took it) — File stays mouse-only (its actions keep Ctrl+O/S/N). `find_sequence` is `priority=True` (sibling of `Ctrl+/` `find_annotation`, which searches feature ANNOTATIONS, not bases) with a self-screen guard so a second Ctrl+F can't stack another modal. `n`/`N` are NON-priority so they don't fire inside Inputs (you can type `n` in the query box). **Shift+n delivery quirk (verified empirically):** a standard terminal delivers Shift+n as the bare uppercase `"N"` (what Textual fires), NOT `"shift+n"` — only kitty / enhanced-keyboard protocols emit `"shift+n"`; bound BOTH so find-previous works on every terminal. **(E) Hardening:** mode-guard on the column→bp helper (circular → no nav, defense-in-depth over the `_align_at_click` gate); stale-search guards — `_goto_search_hit` drops the search if the sequence LENGTH changed mid-search, and `_apply_record(clear_undo=True)` clears `_seq_search` on a fresh canvas swap (catches a same-length plasmid swap the length-guard would miss); modal-stacking guard; matcher rejects empty / oversize query, negative `k`, and (via the normaliser) foreign chars. **Test-harness note:** monkeypatching live `post_message` / `push_screen` on a running app DEADLOCKS `run_test` teardown (broke the message pump) — assert click-nav via the screen stack + cursor instead. Tests: `test_seq_search_nav.py` (42 — matcher exact/RC/k-mismatch/IUPAC/circular-wrap/linear-no-wrap/palindrome-dedup/foreign-raise/RNA/FASTA/sort/cap; `focus_span` cursor/selection/single-bp/clamp/empty; click-nav span-helper / mode+margin reject / handler / missing-panel / on_click-navigates-not-drills-in; find-flow store+jump / next+prev wrap / no-search-safe / stale-guard / cursor-start / `n`+`N` keystrokes-fire / record-swap-clears / no-stack; binding remap incl. `N`). README (Features + Sequencing) + Help shortcut table + CHANGELOG updated. |

Wired into the TWO save chokepoints — `LibraryPanel.add_entry` (generic Save / Synthesis / paste / file import) and `_commit_library_entry_to_collection` (PCR amplicon + universal workbench safety net). The rich workbench builders (`_build_history_for_assembly` / `_build_history_for_product` / `_build_history_for_gibson`) set `history_xml` BEFORE these, so the fallback no-ops for them. Root topology comes from `_gb_text_is_circular(gb_text)` (LOCUS-line read). **Move/copy-to-collection is EXCLUDED** — `_move_copy_commit` saves via `_save_collections` with per-entry deepcopy, never through the chokepoints, so a copy keeps the source's history and never mints a spurious root (user: "aside from moving the plasmid file to another location"). **Constructor enzyme-gate fix:** `_build_history_for_assembly` no longer returns None when `_enzyme_for_level_up` can't resolve an enzyme (it dropped ALL history over a missing detail — the demo311-class bug); the enzyme is now just an optional `RegeneratedSite` marker. **In-place edit append (user choice "append on actual change"):** `_maybe_append_edit_history` adds an `editSequence` node chaining the pre-edit version ONLY when a same-id re-save's SEQUENCE actually changed (cheap `len` check first; same-length → parse-and-compare the prior record); a no-op / features-only re-save adds nothing. **Bloat cap (history lives in BOTH plasmid_library.json AND collections.json; the edit chain re-nests the whole prior tree per edit):** `_truncate_history_to_recent(root, _HISTORY_TREE_MAX_NODES=256)` BFS-keeps the recent window + one `(earlier history truncated)` leaf; a prior history > `_HISTORY_XML_MAX_BYTES` (256 KB) collapses to a leaf instead of being re-parsed; a final byte ceiling drops to the bare edit node. Imported `.dna` history is NOT capped here — its own 32 MB `_COMMERCIALSAAS_HISTORY_MAX_XML` ceiling governs it. **Legacy backfill (CRITICAL data-safety):** `_backfill_origin_history(entries)` is ADDITIVE-ONLY — only ADDS `history_xml` where absent, NEVER removes/reorders/rewrites any entry or other field; one corrupt row is skipped (per-entry try/except), not dropped; idempotent. Wired into BOTH `_ensure_library_cache_populated_and_migrated` and `_ensure_collections_cache_populated_and_migrated` (embedded plasmids) under separate flags (`_origin_history_backfill_done`, `_collections_origin_history_backfill_done`; conftest force-sets both True so tests opt in). Each pass asserts the entry COUNT is invariant before persisting and ABORTS the whole save on any change — belt on top of `_save_*`'s L3 catastrophic-shrink guard + `.bak`/atomic chain; save failure is non-fatal (cache migrated, disk catches up next save). SnapGene/.dna history import verified intact. **History-viewer protocol clarity (2026-06-01 follow-up):** `_history_step_from_node` collects ALL regenerated-site enzymes (was `sites[0]` only — a KpnI+XbaI double digest showed just "✂ KpnI"; verified on real BATCH 55-C data), deduping + dropping the `(reverse insert)` sentinel. `_history_protocol_lines` now reads left → right like a recipe — `<op>  <ingredients> into <backbone>  →  <product>  ✂ <enzymes>` — with the forward arrow pointing AT the product (replacing the unclear backward `⟵`); an in-place edit (input == product) collapses to one labelled product. `_history_protocol_text` prepends a one-line symbol legend (shown in both viewers). `_HISTORY_OP_FRIENDLY` gained `amplifyFragment→PCR` + `editSequence→edit`. Tests: `test_origin_history.py` (41 — helpers, descriptor map, enzyme-gate, edit-append, bloat cap over 400 edits, additive backfill + count-invariant + idempotent + collections-embedded); `test_commercialsaas_io.py` protocol tests (all-enzymes, dedup/sentinel, legend present). **Protocol readability + harmonised history (2026-06-01 follow-ups):** the protocol pane renders a Rich `Group(legend, 2-col table)` via `_history_protocol_renderable` (`_history_protocol_step_cells` splits number from content) so a wrapped step HANGS-INDENTS under the content, not the next step's number. Amplify (PCR) detail: `_CommercialSaaSHistoryNode.oligos` parses `<Oligo>` primer children + `add_oligo` writes them; `_history_detail_lines` shows a **Primers** block (name + sequence) and the amplified **region** (`val1/val2`) instead of a bare "amplify ? ↔ ?" (SnapGene's amplify `InputSummary` carries no name1/name2 — the detail was in the `.dna` `<Oligo>`/`Parameter` children all along). **Uniform history regardless of origin:** `_build_amplicon_history_xml` makes a de-novo SpliceCraft amplicon record the SAME element set a `.dna` import does — an `amplifyFragment` node + `amplify` region `InputSummary` + primer `<Oligo>`s — wired into `SimulatorScreen._build_amplicon_library_entry` (was a bare `simulator:pcr` origin root). This propagates: when an amplicon is a part in a GB/Gibson/traditional assembly, `_parent_node_for_entry` nests its oligo-bearing subtree, so primers survive through multi-step builds. (Not yet generated: the cosmetic `.dna` `HistoryColors` / redundant `Features` / PCR `Parameter` elements.) |
| `[INV-92]` | Nuanced bar-mode alignment overlay shading (2026-06-01). Replaces the flat worst-state collapse (`[INV-87]`'s `_alignment_bar_columns`) for the zoomed-out (< 1 col/bp) linear overlay with a per-column composition SHADE so the blue/red/gray patchwork survives at full zoom-out (user: "see where it binds and where it doesnt … that granularity at max zoom too"). `_alignment_bar_column_shades(segments, view_s, view_e, bp_to_col, lo, hi)` accumulates per-column bp COUNTS `{col: (n_match, n_mismatch, n_gap)}` — walked per-bp over the visible window, so a lone mismatch bp is counted in its column alongside the surrounding match majority (no degenerate-range drop). `_alignment_shade_cell(n_m, n_x, n_g)` → `(glyph, rich_style)`: gap-dominant (`n_g > n_m + n_x`) → gray `░`; pure match → blue `█`; else a red shade glyph on a blue bg whose density tracks the mismatch fraction among ALIGNED bp via `_ALIGN_SHADE_TIERS` (`░` < 0.125, `▒` < 0.375, `▓` < 0.625), tipping to solid red `█` ≥ 0.625. So a single SNP in a ~164 bp cell → faint `░` red-on-blue fleck (visible, yet clearly mostly-binding); a half-mismatch cell → `▓`; a divergent read → solid red; a perfect read → solid blue. `_draw_alignment_band`'s bar branch derives the name-overlay `col_state` (gap-dominant→`gap` / any-mismatch→`mismatch` / else `match`) from the same counts. `_alignment_bar_columns` (worst-state) is retained for its tests but no longer drives the paint. Verified on real DEMO 40 stored alignments: DEMO40-4 (1 SNP) → mostly-blue bar with one `░` at the SNP column; the three 100% reads → solid blue; the 52% read → uniform `▒`. Tests: `TestAlignmentShadeCell` (11), `TestAlignmentBarColumnShades` (6). |
| `[INV-91]` | edlib fast-aligner engine (2026-05-31). `_pairwise_align`'s **global**-mode engine is now edlib (bit-parallel Myers edit distance) when available, falling back to Biopython otherwise. ~90–600× faster on the long near-identical sequences that drive QC: `_pick_best_rotation` on a real 18 kb Plasmidsaurus read drops from ~16–48 s to **~0.33 s**, with IDENTICAL identity / mismatch / indel counts (validated base-for-base on JP4W9V; edlib diverges from Biopython ONLY on low-identity alignments, which the rotation picker discards — and where the exact count is immaterial since the read is flagged divergent either way). `_edlib_align_global(q, t)` returns the gapped `(query, target)` strings; `_pairwise_align` keeps its IUPAC-aware counting (`_iupac_compatible`) AND hands the same IUPAC pairs to edlib as `additionalEqualities` (`_EDLIB_IUPAC_EQUALITIES` — 88 pairs built once from `_iupac_compatible`) so the alignment engine and the match counting agree on ambiguity (N vs A, R vs G, …). `score` is read off the Biopython row when that path runs, else reconstructed from the counts with the same scalar params (display-only — the picker ranks by identity, `[INV-76]`). edlib is OPTIONAL (`_EDLIB_AVAILABLE`, `# type: ignore[import-not-found]`) with a `sys_platform != 'win32'` marker in pyproject (mirrors pyhmmer — edlib's Windows wheels are unreliable, and the Biopython fallback covers it) plus a dev-dep so CI exercises the edlib path while `release.py`'s system-python run exercises the fallback. **Local mode stays on Biopython** (edlib has no local-SW equivalent; no caller passes `mode="local"` in practice). This makes the bulk-align process-parallelism idea moot — per-read align is now ~sub-second, so a whole run is seconds. **Hardening (rock-solid):** the edlib path is wrapped per-call — any edlib exception OR a **round-trip-guard** failure (the ungapped aligned rows MUST reconstruct the normalised inputs `q`/`t`; if not, edlib mis-aligned) logs a debug breadcrumb and falls back to Biopython for that single call, so a corrupt / mis-ordered edlib result can never reach the QC counts (Biopython is the universal safety net, reached via `if aligned_q is None`). `_log_startup_banner` records `edlib : <ver> (active …)` / `not installed — Biopython fallback` for the diagnostic bundle; the reconstructed score clamps its gap term (`max(0, …)`). Tests: `TestPairwiseAlignEngine` (8) — fallback-when-disabled, round-trip-guard-falls-back, edlib-exception-falls-back, round-trip-reconstructs-inputs, IUPAC-ambiguity-as-match, empty/oversized-rejected (all engine-agnostic via monkeypatch), + edlib≡Biopython near-identical equivalence and the `_edlib_align_global` round-trip (skip without edlib). All existing `_pairwise_align` / `_pick_best_rotation` suites pass under BOTH engines (4620 full-suite + 389 alignment-file under edlib; 387+2-skip under Biopython). |

The AlignmentManager "Gaps" column and the bulk-confirm `_aln["gaps"]` now use it instead of `n_gap_cols` (gapped bp), so all three QC surfaces agree: a 5 bp deletion reads "1" in the Manager "Gaps", the bulk "Gaps", AND the VerificationReportModal "Indels" (which counts the same gap runs via `_extract_variants_from_alignment`). Mism/SNPs already agreed (each mismatch column is one SNP). Tests: `TestAlignmentIndelEvents` (7, incl. a `_extract_variants_from_alignment` equivalence lock). |

The collection mirror (`_sync_active_collection_plasmids`, which clones its arg) uses the same snapshot. (4) Agent library RMW handlers `_h_delete_from_library`/`_h_set_plasmid_status`/`_h_add_current_to_library` (+ `LibraryPanel.add_entry`'s id-match / no-collision / `_on_resolved` commit paths) now hold `_cache_lock` across load→mutate→save — UI-thread serialisation does NOT exclude worker-thread `_cache_lock` holders (e.g. `_h_gibson_assemble`), so a lockless `_load_library()` could be clobbered. Unfixed [INV-74] instances. **Privacy [INV-38]:** `_extract_recent_events` scrubs every top-level string field value via `_scrub_path`, so the F9 bundle's `events_summary.json` can't leak home/data-dir paths (the human-readable log copies were already scrubbed; `threading.excepthook` doesn't cover pool workers) now toasts + logs (`worker.error` event) instead of a silently frozen panel. `_user_data_dir` mkdir + `Path.home()` wrapped — a misconfigured `$SPLICECRAFT_DATA_DIR` (a file / non-dir path) or unset `$HOME` no longer hard-crashes at IMPORT; falls back to a temp dir with an stderr notice. `main()` rejects a leading-dash positional (`splicecraft --bogus`) with exit(2) instead of handing it to `fetch_genbank` (confusing "Fetch failed"). `_enzyme_cuts_impl` try/excepts `_iupac_pattern(site_u)` per enzyme — a corrupt `custom_enzymes.json` site no longer crashes trad-cloning Simulate / MoClo `@work` digests (parity with [INV-85]'s `_rebuild_scan_catalog` guard). `_gibson_normalize_fragments` maps RNA `U`→`T`. `_parse_pypi_version` requires pure-ASCII-digit segments (rejects `"+5"`/`"1_0"`/Unicode digits/empty). `_resolve_pypi_url` redacts override-URL credentials in warning logs. **Security:** `_hmmer_web_parse_json` strips control bytes from `clan`/`type`/`link` (the gap acc/name/desc strips left); `_online_render_blast`/`_online_render_hmm` wrap remote text cells in `Text(...)` so DataTable renders them LITERALLY (Rich-markup-injection defense); `_online_http` now routes through `_build_hardened_url_opener` (https-only, bounded redirects, https→http downgrade refusal) — was the default global opener; `_fetch_hmm_db_remote_version` bounds gzip decompression to `_HMM_DB_VERSION_MAX_BYTES` via streamed `GzipFile.read(cap+1)` (gzip-bomb defense; pre-fix one-shot `gzip.decompress`); `_normalise_experiment_entry` strips `body_md` (`_NOTE_CTRL_RE`) + tags (`_sanitize_label`); `_normalise_gel_entry` routes notes through `_sanitize_note`. **Genetic codes (wrong-biology fix):** `_codon_table_for(table_id)` resolves the codon→AA map for any NCBI genetic-code id (Biopython `unambiguous_dna_by_id`, cached; A vertebrate-mito / Mycoplasma / Spiroplasma CDS now translates correctly and a reassigned stop (e.g. `TGA`→Trp in tables 2/4/5) no longer false-flags a premature-stop ⚠. `_find_orfs` + `_mut_translate` deliberately stay table-1 (ORFs are de-novo; the mutagenesis WT-check is a niche-of-niche). settings-flush broad-except now `_bg_notify_save_failure`s; 5 modal-open `_load_library`/`_load_collections` deepcopies → readonly iterators (`BlastModal`, `MultiAlignPickerModal`, `MoveCopyToCollectionModal`, Mutagenize select, `AddFeatureModal._import`). **Completeness (new/wired):** ORF finder reachable via File → "Find ORFs in this sequence…"; (g) cancel-aware (`cancel_check_cb` polled between chunks; modal close = cancellation); (h) `_redact_url_credentials` on every logged URL. **Atomic state:** download → `db.hmm.gz.download_tmp` → fsync → `os.replace`; ## [INV-36] Future-proofing scaffolding
Six additive mechanisms:
* `_ENTRY_MIGRATIONS` per-label `(from_v, to_v) → Callable[[dict], dict]`. `_extract_entries` runs every load through `_migrate_entries`. Failed migrators preserve entry + warn (never drop data).
* `$SPLICECRAFT_PYPI_URL` env override (http/https only, ≤2048 chars). No caching.
* Pre-update snapshots record `from_python_version` + `from_platform`. **`_RUNTIME_PLATFORM` cached at import** because `platform.platform()` shells out via subprocess.
* `--dry-run` exercises detection/PyPI/snapshot then bails. Mutex with `--check`.
* `<DATA_DIR>/.splicecraft-data-version` stamp; `_check_and_stamp_data_version()` warns to stderr on downgrade. Atomic write via `_atomic_write_text`, read capped at 128 bytes.
* `_PLUGINS_DIR = _DATA_DIR / "plugins"` + `_RESERVED_ENTRY_FIELDS = ("_plugin_data",)`. Tested by `TestFutureProofingFeatures`.

## [INV-37] Robustness pass (0.7.6)
Tested by `TestRobustnessHardening`:
* `_acquire_data_dir_lock` (POSIX `fcntl.flock` / Win `msvcrt.locking`) at `<DATA_DIR>/splicecraft.lock`; PID-carrying; `$SPLICECRAFT_SKIP_LOCK=1` bypass.
* `threading.excepthook` → `_log.error`. `_chmod_user_only` 0o600 on logs/bundles. `_drain_in_flight_workers(timeout_s=2.0)` in `main()` finally.
* `_SETTINGS_SCHEMA` + `_validate_settings` — **strict bool-vs-int** (`True` does NOT coerce into `int`). Unknown keys pass through.
* Network retry: 1 try + 250 ms backoff on `_fetch_latest_pypi_version` + `fetch_genbank`. 4-tier `_copy_to_clipboard_with_fallback`: Textual → OSC 52 → `<DATA_DIR>/clipboard/<ts>-<label>.txt` → log.
* `_MODAL_STACK_SOFT_CAP = 12` on `push_screen` with `callback(None)` fallback. `_apply_record` notifies > `_LARGE_PLASMID_BP = 5_000_000`. `_snapshot_data_files` skips > `_SNAPSHOT_FILE_SIZE_CAP = 50 MB`.

## [INV-38] Diagnostic logging + UI snapshot + bundle
Three surfaces:
* Rotating log `<DATA_DIR>/logs/splicecraft.log` (`RotatingFileHandler`, 5 MB × 4 backups, 8-char `_SESSION_ID` prefix). **NEVER log sequence content** — `_repr_for_log` truncates/summarises.
* **`Alt+D`** App-priority → `action_capture_ui_snapshot` → `<DATA_DIR>/ui_snapshots/ui-snapshot-<ts>.md` (version, Python, platform, screen stack, focused widget, terminal size, record metadata excl. sequence, settings, 200-line log tail with `/home/<user>` → `~`). `_collect_ui_snapshot` defensive. Retention `_UI_SNAPSHOT_RETENTION = 20`. Old hover-debug moved to `alt+shift+d`.
* `splicecraft logs --bundle [--out PATH]` zips logs + last 5 snapshots + sanitized settings + system info + README. `_scrub_path` handles `/home/<user>`, `/Users/<user>`, `C:\Users\<user>`, `Path.home()`. **Sacred privacy invariant: sequence content MUST never leak.**

## [INV-39] `splicecraft update` snapshots user data before any install subprocess
All upgrade paths (pipx/uv-tool/uv-venv/pixi-global/pip-user/pip-venv) call `_create_pre_update_snapshot(__version__)` AFTER user confirm BEFORE `subprocess.run`. Covers `_USER_DATA_FILE_ATTRS` (20 entries — library, collections, parts_bin, primers, features, feature_colors, grammars, entry_vectors, codon_tables, settings, … see `splicecraft_backup._USER_DATA_FILE_ATTRS` for the full live list) + `_USER_DATA_DIR_ATTRS` (crash_recovery, dna_originals). **Atomic**: built in `<backup_dir>/.tmp-<rand>/`, fsynced, sealed by `os.replace`. Failure → staging removed + `OSError`/`shutil.Error` raised → exit 1. Location is **sibling** `<DATA_DIR>/../<DATA_DIR.name>-update-backups/` (override `$SPLICECRAFT_UPDATE_BACKUP_DIR`) so recursive-wipe can't kill recovery. Refuses when `_data_dir_inside_install_path()`. Restore: `splicecraft update --restore-pre-update [<id>|latest]` takes pre-restore snapshot first. Retention 5; rmtree restricted to `_PRE_UPDATE_NAME_RE`. **Sacred four restore checks**: `schema_version` ≤ `_PRE_UPDATE_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `attr` in whitelist, `name` rejects separators/`..`, SHA-256 re-verified before `os.replace`. Refusal paths + `--check` MUST NOT snapshot.

## [INV-40] Overhang pair is sacred source of truth for part classification
`_classify_part_from_plasmid` resolves part type/level/position **purely** from the `(oh5, oh3)` pair released by each grammar's primary/secondary Type IIS enzyme. Feature labels, plasmid name, source filename NEVER consulted. Returns `None` if pair doesn't match any position (with "couldn't classify — use New Part" notify). Grammar position-table changes affect "this pair now/no longer classifies"; never re-route via heuristics. Adding GB 2.0 expanded grammar was a position-table change, not a classifier change.

## [INV-41] Robustness sweeps #2–#6 (0.7.5→0.8.10)
Key invariants:
* **`_feat_bounds(feat, total) → (start, end, strand)`** is the canonical wrap-aware extractor. Use instead of raw `int(loc.start)/.end`.
* **`_smallest_enclosing_feature(bp)`** (bisect on `_feats_starts_sorted` + wrap pass) replaces O(N) `_feat_at` for bp-lookup.
* **Worker pattern:** heavy ops use `@work(exclusive=True, group=...)`. UI thread pre-captures inputs (`_collect_*_inputs`); worker emits `_on_*_failed` + `_apply_*_result`; never touches `widget.update`. Workers capture `_record_load_counter` at entry (extends invariant #28).
* **Modal Ctrl+Z:** `_blocks_undo: bool = True` opts modal out of app-level undo. **Attr must come AFTER docstring** or first-statement detection breaks. Applied to Constructor/Domesticator/Mutagenize/PrimerDuplicates/PrimerDesignScreen/PartsBinModal/FeatureLibraryScreen.
* **Primer dedup modal** defaults to KEEP (focus+Esc → Keep); `_skip_primer_dedupe_check` test flag.
* **Atomic writes:** `_atomic_write_bytes(path, data)` is byte-mode counterpart to `_atomic_write_text`; all `.bak`/`.bak.<ts>`/daily-snapshot copies route through it. `.dna`/bundle/token writers call `_fsync_parent_dir` after `os.replace`. Same-second collision protector bumps `.bak.<ts>.{N}.json`.
* **Symlink refusal:** `_safe_save_json` via `path.is_symlink()`; `_check_agent_read_dir` via `lstat`+`S_ISDIR`; `_check_agent_write_path` walks FULL ancestor chain via `resolve()` divergence + per-segment `is_symlink()`.
* **`.dna` sidecar case-collision fix (HIGH).** `_dna_sidecar_path` case-folds basename + appends 8-char SHA-1 prefix of raw `entry_id`. `_dna_sidecar_legacy_path` migrates on first read. `_DNA_SIDECAR_BASENAME_MAX = 200`.
* **Pre-update restore.** `_restore_pre_update_snapshot` REFUSES when manifest `sha256` missing/empty. Manifest reads capped at `_PRE_UPDATE_MANIFEST_MAX_BYTES = 4 MB`. `_restore_from_backup` staging uses `tempfile.mkstemp`.
* **Agent-API save uniformity.** `_LIVE_APP_REF` soft pointer set in `on_mount` + `_agent_dispatch`, cleared in `finally`. `_agent_save_or_500(save_fn, label)` wraps every agent-endpoint `_save_*`; `_bg_notify_save_failure(label, exc)` daemon-thread counterpart.
* **Concurrency.** Module-level `_cache_lock = threading.RLock()` wraps `_safe_save_json` + cache-reassignment in every JSON save helper. RLock because chains nest (`_save_library` ⇒ `_sync_active_collection_plasmids` ⇒ `_save_collections`). Reads don't take lock (`_typed_clone`-on-return + GIL). `_settings_flush_worker` try/finally so exception can't wedge `_settings_flush_running=True`. HMMscan/BLAST run/BLAST build split into distinct `@work(group=...)`.
* **Defence-in-depth size caps.** `_gb_text_to_record` > `_GB_TEXT_MAX_BYTES = 64 MB`. `_h_hmmscan` via `_safe_file_size_check` (2 GB). `_backup_info` + `_restore_from_backup` + `.bak` fallback via 1 GB cap. `_h_diff_plasmid` pre-caps both seqs at `_PAIRWISE_MAX_LEN`. Export endpoints whitelist via `_check_export_extension`. `_sanitize_path` refuses `~user`. `splicecraft_cli.py` caps body (50 MB) + token (1 KB).
* **Hygiene.** `_sweep_orphan_tmp_files` collects `.tmp`/`.migrating`/`.restoring` >1 h old. Lockfile uses `O_EXCL`. PID-alive recheck on stale lock.
* **`MultiAlignPickerModal`** ships `(entry_id, gb_text)` tuples so multi-Mb dismiss doesn't block UI.
* `tests/test_sweep5.py` (21 cases) regression coverage.

## [INV-42] Structured event logging (0.8.7+)
Design: **user pastes log → AI parses → patch shipped same loop**. `_log_event(event, *, _stacklevel=2, **fields)` emits `event <namespaced.name> {JSON}`. Strings >200 chars truncated with `…[+N]`; `SeqRecord`/`Seq`/`MutableSeq`/`bytes`/`bytearray` rendered as opaque tags (`<SeqRecord id=X len=Y>`) via class-name match (no BioPython import) — accidental `_log_event('e', rec=record)` cannot leak BioPython's `__repr__`-embedded bases. `seq.chunk_dump` only logs via structured event; `seq.hover_copy` logs `text_len` not `text`; UI snapshot routes settings through `_scrub_path`. `@_action_log(event_name)` decorates every `action_*` (exceptions swallowed — **logging must never break the underlying action**). `@_timed(path, threshold_ms=0)` wraps heavy ops; emits `op.timed`. Decorators pass `_stacklevel=3` so `funcName:lineno` lands on wrapped method. Names: `app.<area>.<verb>` (user actions), `op.<area>.<verb>` (ops), `<noun>.<verb>` (state — `save.ok`/`save.failed`, `undo.*`, `record.loaded`, `collection.switched`, `settings.changed`, `migration.step/failed`, `net.retry`, `lock.acquired/contended/stale/released`, `shutdown.drain.ok/timeout`). `SPLICECRAFT_DEBUG=1` bumps to DEBUG.

## [INV-43] Adversarial sweep #9 (2026-05-19, pre-v1.0.0)
11 HIGH + 10 MEDIUM fixes; `tests/test_sweep9.py` (50 cases). Key invariants:
* **Cache-bust on pre-update restore** (`_h_restore_pre_update_snapshot`) must enumerate **every** persisted-state cache including `_experiments_cache`, `_experiment_projects_cache`, `_gels_cache`. `RestoreFromBackupModal._TARGETS` mirrors.
* **Project-switch atomicity.** `ExperimentProjectsPickerModal._open` AND `_do_delete` active-promotion MUST: (a) update active-pointer via `_set_active_project_name`, (b) FORCE sync flush via `_settings_flush_sync()`, (c) write `experiments.json` for promoted project. Pre-fix left power-loss window where settings said OLD and experiments held NEW.
* **App-level Ctrl+Z routes through public `action_undo`** (NOT private `_action_undo`) so modal `_blocks_undo` actually fires.
* **`_save_experiments` mirror inside `_cache_lock`.** `_sync_active_project_experiments(entries)` MUST be inside `with _cache_lock:` block — RLock allows nested `_save_experiment_projects` re-acquire.
* **`_check_data_files` covers every persisted file** (sweep #9 added experiments/experiment_projects/gels/parts_bin_collections).
* **`_sweep_orphan_tmp_files` walks `_EXPERIMENTS_DIR` per-entry subdirs** one level deep with symlink refusal at each entry.
* **All new modals over editor/text input carry `_blocks_undo: bool = True`** (attr AFTER docstring). Sweep #9 added to `ExperimentProjectsPickerModal`, `ExperimentDeleteConfirmModal`, `ExperimentUnsavedChangesModal`, `ExperimentRenameModal`, `GelLibraryModal`, `ActionsPickerModal`, `ImageAttachModal`.
* **Tag regex atomic-group hardening.** `_PLASMID_REF_RE`/`_ACTIONS_REF_RE`/`_GEL_REF_RE` use `(?=([A-Za-z][\w.\-]{0,63}))\1(?![;=])` lookahead+backref to prevent backtracking (Python 3.10 lacks possessive / atomic group). Without it, `&amp;`/URL params would partial-match.
* **Windows-zip separator resilience.** `_zf_get_member_info(zf, name)` tries exact, falls back to normalised scan of `zf.infolist()` (Plasmidsaurus zips on Windows ship `category\\sample.gbk`).
* **SequencingScreen same-path cache** invalidates on content drift via `_zip_signature: tuple[mtime_ns, size]`.
* **Lockfile staleness via argv.** `_pid_is_splicecraft(pid)` reads `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` on POSIX to detect PID-recycle. Returns None where unimplementable. Stale-lock log carries `reason="no_live_process"`/`"pid_recycled_to_unrelated"`.
* **Clipboard image megapixel cap.** `_EXPERIMENT_CLIP_MAX_PIXELS = 50_000_000` checked BEFORE `img.save`. Save in try/finally so failure unlinks tmpfile.
* **`_summarize_perbase_tsv` float-aware** — `float()` not `int()`; extraction `int(float(cols[2]))`.
* **Legacy tag migration runs on every save** (`_normalise_experiment_entry` triggers `_migrate_legacy_tag_format` when `@plasmid:`/`@actions:` substrings present).
* **`_SETTINGS_SCHEMA` covers every persisted key** (sweep #9 added `active_parts_bin`, `active_project`, `experiments_custom_dict`).
* **Highlight-map per-line caps.** `_HIGHLIGHT_LINE_LEN_CAP = 50_000` / `_HIGHLIGHT_PER_LINE_TAG_CAP = 500` on `_ExperimentMarkdownTextArea._build_highlight_map`.
* **`_confirm_delete` (Experiments) resolves cursor row FIRST**, falls back to loaded entry only when no cursor. Arrow keys don't fire `_on_entry_selected`.
* **Plasmid cross-ref click prompts on unsaved canvas edits.** `_open_plasmid_ref` wraps switch+load with `UnsavedNavigateModal` when `app._unsaved`.

## [INV-44] Every save is a full plasmid (2026-05-19)
User contract: every assembly path — L0 Domesticator, Constructor TU / MOD / L3+, Traditional cloning, Gibson, MoClo — lands as a library entry whose `gb_text` is a single complete circular plasmid carrying **payload + overhangs + backbone** AND every parent-fragment feature as its own annotation. Coverage closed in `TestEverySaveIsAFullPlasmid` (11 cases):
* **L0 Domesticator → library mirror.** `PartsBinModal._new_part._on_result` runs the parts-bin save sync, then dispatches `_domesticator_library_mirror_worker` (`@work(thread=True, exclusive=True, group="dom_library_mirror")`) which calls `_part_to_cloned_seqrecord(part_dict)` and saves the full part-in-entry-vector plasmid as a library entry. **Off-thread** so a 100+ MB library doesn't freeze the UI between bin save and the success notify; mirror is best-effort (clone / save failure logs + notifies but does NOT roll back the bin row).
* **Parts Bin Save-to-Collection for L1+.** `_save_to_collection` filter accepts rows with `sequence` OR `gb_text` (was: `sequence` only — rejected every TU / MOD silently). `_part_to_cloned_seqrecord` short-circuits at **tier 0** for L1+: `gb_text` IS the cloned plasmid, parse and return it directly. Tier 0 validates `_seq_len(rec) > 0` and falls through on a zero-length record (corrupt data) so the explicit "no sequence" ValueError surfaces instead of a degenerate 0-bp library row. `_diagnose_part_cloning` returns None for every L1+ row (with OR without gb_text) since the IIS-vector diagnostics don't apply.
* **Colour preservation in clone simulation.** `_clone_part_marshal_vec_features` reads `ApEinfo_fwdcolor`/`ApEinfo_revcolor`/`color` from vector features (was hardcoded `"white"`). `_clone_part_build_seqrecord` writes `ApEinfo_fwdcolor`/`ApEinfo_revcolor` qualifiers when the carried colour is non-white. `_clone_part_build_part_feature` sources L0 part colour from `_GB_TYPE_COLORS[part_type]` (was hardcoded `"white"`) so Domesticator → library entries are visually typed.
* **Wrap-feature `CompoundLocation` in `_clone_part_build_seqrecord`.** Features with `end < start` render as `CompoundLocation([(s, n_seq), (0, e)])` — origin-spanning backbone features (e.g. an Ori straddling the relegated join) survive instead of being silently dropped by the old `if e <= s: continue` guard.
* **Structured save events for parity with Gibson.** `constructor.save.ok` / `constructor.save.failed` fired from `_save_to_library_worker` (fields: `gid`, `source_level`, `target_level`, `name`, `id`, `bp`, `n_feats`, `parts`, `backbone` / `stage`, `error`). `traditional.save.ok` / `traditional.save.failed` fired from `_save_product_to_library`. `domesticator.library_mirror.ok` / `domesticator.library_mirror.failed` fired from the worker. Naming follows the `<noun>.save.<verb>` convention `gibson.save.ok` established.

## [INV-45] Cross-platform contract (2026-05-19)
SpliceCraft runs on every modern OS / terminal pairing listed in `docs/PLATFORMS.md`. Audit sweep: `tests/test_cross_platform.py` (11 cases) covers every `sys.platform` branch via monkeypatch. Key invariants:
* **UTF-8 stdout encoding is blocking.** `_check_terminal_capabilities()` refuses to launch on non-UTF-8 / non-Unicode terminals (the braille map U+2800-U+28FF would render as gibberish) with an actionable error pointing at `PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8` / `LANG=C.UTF-8`. Aliases `utf8`, `cp65001` (Windows Terminal in `chcp 65001`), `utf-16`, `utf-32` all pass. Empty encoding (piped) is silently skipped.
* **Optional Python deps degrade, never block.** `PIL` (clipboard image), `primer3` (Tm), `pyspellchecker` (F7) absence emits warnings via `_log.warning` + `_log_event("startup.terminal_capabilities", warning_count=N)` but launch proceeds. Per-feature gates handle the missing-import case at the call site.
* **OSC 52 clipboard target is OS-specific.** `_copy_to_clipboard_osc52` writes to `/dev/tty` on POSIX, `CONOUT$` on Windows. Either failure (no controlling terminal, no console output device, encoding error) returns False so the multi-tier wrapper escalates to the file-based tier-3 fallback.
* **Signal handlers gated by `hasattr` / `getattr`.** `SIGUSR1` (POSIX-only) faulthandler install: `if hasattr(_signal, "SIGUSR1")`. `SIGTERM` / `SIGHUP` translation: `getattr(_signal, name, None)`. Windows missing `SIGHUP` is fine.
* **Subprocess calls always list-form, never `shell=True`.** Cross-platform quoting parity for the update install path + every other shell-out.
* **`_RUNTIME_PLATFORM` cached at import** because `platform.platform()` shells out via subprocess on some OSes (conflicts with tests monkeypatching `subprocess.run`). Same cache pattern as `[INV-36]`.
* **Stale lock detection has POSIX-specific path.** `_pid_is_splicecraft(pid)` reads `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` on Linux to detect PID-recycle to unrelated processes. Returns None on macOS / Windows (no `/proc`); the conservative "assume live" stance preserves correctness.
* **`/dev/tty` / `CONOUT$` writes (OSC 52) wrapped in `try / except (OSError, UnicodeEncodeError)`** so a broken SSH session / WSL-without-TTY / Windows legacy `conhost.exe` never crashes the clipboard helper.

## [INV-47] Entry-vector auto-detection (2026-05-20)
`_detect_entry_vector_role(record, grammar)` identifies which entry-vector role a plasmid plays via Type IIS digest fingerprinting — NOT name/label heuristics. Strategy: digest with both grammar enzymes, identify stuffer via `_fragment_has_backbone_marker` exclusion (NEVER size — `feedback_never_assume_smaller_frag_is_payload`), match `(inner enzyme, outer overhang pair)` → role via Golden Braid binary-assembly architecture. Returns `(role, "strict")` for α/Ω acceptors whose inner digest matches the canonical TU boundary AND outer digest matches a recognised pair; `("", "weak")` for UPD-style L0 donors with non-canonical stuffers; `None` for L0 parts, TUs, MODs, or anything else. `_resolve_acceptor_role(grammar, inner_enz, outer_pair)` carries the gb_l0 mapping (`Alpha1=Esp3I-outer-(GGAG,GTCA)`, `Alpha2=Esp3I-outer-(GTCA,CGCT)`, `Omega1=BsaI-outer-(GGAG,GTCA)`, `Omega2=BsaI-outer-(GTCA,CGCT)`). `_auto_bind_entry_vectors_from_entries(entries)` runs detection across every grammar for a batch of library entries, binds new roles via `_set_entry_vector` (NEVER clobbers existing user bindings; strict > weak on collisions). Surfaced two ways:
* **Auto on collection import** — `_bulk_import_folder` callers append the auto-bind summary onto the import success notification. Zero-click happy path: user imports their DemoColl/FFE folder, classification works immediately.
* **Settings → Entry Vectors…** — `EntryVectorsModal` (`_blocks_undo=True`) shows every role for the active grammar in one table with Pick/Clear/Auto-detect controls. Replaces the single-slot widget in `GrammarEditorModal` (which only exposed the empty-role binding). Grammar selector at top lets the user reconfigure for any grammar. Default focus on the table so arrow keys + Enter drive the picks. Esc → close.
* Tested by `tests/test_entry_vectors_auto.py` (17 cases) covering α1/α2/Ω1/Ω2 strict detection, UPD weak match, rejection of L0 parts / TUs / linear records / marker-less plasmids, auto-bind precedence (strict > weak), no-clobber guarantee, modal smoke test.

## [INV-48] Master Delete (2026-05-20)
File menu → "⚠ Master Delete (wipe all user data)…" wipes every persistent SpliceCraft file the user can produce — library, collections, parts bin, parts-bin collections, primers, features, feature colours, custom grammars, entry vectors, codon tables, settings, experiments, experiment projects, gels, agent token, data-version stamp, all `.bak` / `.bak.<ts>` siblings, daily snapshots, lost-entries spillover, UI snapshots, clipboard tmpfiles, crash-recovery autosaves, `.dna` sidecars, plugins dir, rotated log backups, `.migrated` legacy marker, AND the sibling pre-update-backups directory. True clean slate — no built-in restore path. Tested by `tests/test_master_delete.py` (33 cases). Sacred — two independent blockers against accidental fire:
* **No agent endpoint.** `_AGENT_HANDLERS` has zero handlers whose name contains `master`/`wipe`/`nuke`/`delete_all`/`clean_slate`/`purge`/`destroy`. Enforced by `test_no_agent_endpoint_exposes_wipe`.
* **Module-local sentinel.** `_perform_master_delete(app, *, sentinel)` raises RuntimeError BEFORE any disk op unless `sentinel is _MASTER_DELETE_SENTINEL` (a bare `object()` only the GUI flow holds a reference to). Enforced by `test_sentinel_mismatch_refused_before_any_disk_op`.
* **Typed-YES gate (`MasterDeleteModal`).** Stage 1 Input must read exactly `"YES"` — case-sensitive, no `.strip()` / `.casefold()`. Cancel default-focused; Esc → cancel. Delete button starts disabled and only enables on the exact-match `Input.Changed`. `_blocks_undo = True`.
* **3-second cool-down (`MasterDeleteConfirmModal`).** Stage 2 confirm button starts disabled with a countdown label (`set_interval(0.1, …)` ticks `_cooldown_remaining`) and only enables after `_MASTER_DELETE_CONFIRM_COOLDOWN_S` (3 s) elapses. Default focus on `No, keep my data`. `_blocks_undo = True`.
* **No keyboard binding.** `action_master_delete` is reachable from the File menu only. Enforced by `test_no_keyboard_binding_for_master_delete` (greps the source for `Binding(... master_delete ...)`).
* **Re-entrancy guard.** `PlasmidApp._master_delete_in_progress` flips True at action entry, False in every terminal callback (stage-1 cancel, stage-2 cancel, result-modal dismiss, wipe error). A menu double-click finds the flag set and notifies "already running"; no second flow stacks. Enforced by `test_action_master_delete_reentrancy_guard`.
* **Lockfile + active log preserved.** `_master_delete_residual_paths` resolves both `_DATA_DIR / "splicecraft.lock"` and `_DATA_DIR / "logs"` and excludes them from the residual sweep. The process holding the lock survives the wipe; the `RotatingFileHandler` writes survive to a still-open file. `_master_delete_log_files` separately removes rotated log backups (`splicecraft.log.1`, `.2`, …) while skipping `_LOG_PATH`.
* **Residual sweep (defense in depth).** After the named-target pass, `_master_delete_residual_paths` walks `_DATA_DIR.iterdir()` and unlink/rmtrees every file/dir that isn't the lockfile or logs/. Any future persistence file added without a corresponding `_USER_DATA_FILE_ATTRS` entry still gets wiped. `summary["residual_files"]` / `residual_dirs` counts surface in the result modal.
* **Cache reset under `_cache_lock`.** Every name in `_MASTER_DELETE_CACHE_ATTRS` (14 entries — every `_*_cache` global) reset to None inside the lock, after the file-level deletion but before the lock releases. A concurrent `_save_*` queued behind us thus finds the cache emptied and writes the empty-shape payload onto a never-existing path. `_settings_flush_pending` cleared FIRST so the daemon flush worker doesn't beat the wipe to disk.
* **Agent server stopped first.** `_stop_agent_api(app._agent_api_server)` before the wipe so an authenticated agent session can't re-populate data into the wiped tree. Token file unlinked by `_stop_agent_api` as a side effect.
* **Pre-wipe scope count on main thread.** `action_master_delete` enumerates file/dir targets BEFORE pushing the first modal so both modals + the post-wipe summary quote the same numbers. Scope count failures degrade to zeros (the wipe itself still proceeds).

## [INV-49] Load-time collision resolution (2026-05-20)
Every load path that lands user-named items in a persistent list — parts bin (single + bulk Load Part, Domesticator), plasmid library (`LibraryPanel.add_entry`), primer library (`PrimerDesignScreen._save_primers_btn`) — routes through `_resolve_load_collisions(app, entity, new_items, existing_items, *, name_key, content_fn, on_resolved, on_cancelled)`. Two collision categories, two modals:
* **Exact copy** (same name + same content) → `ExactCopyConfirmModal[bool]`. Default focus on Skip; "Keep as COPY" renames via `_ensure_unique_copy_name` (" COPY" / " COPY 2" / …) so the duplicate can coexist with its twin. Returns True to keep, False/None to skip.
* **Name collision** (same name, different content) → `NameCollisionModal[str]`. Three-way: keep original (default), overwrite, cancel. Cancel aborts the ENTIRE load (caller does NOTHING) — surfaced via `on_cancelled` callback. Keep drops the colliding new items; Overwrite removes existing entries whose name matches and lets the new ones land.
* **Counter-based parts-bin delete** — `PartsBinModal._delete_selected` switched from set-membership on `(name, sequence)` to `Counter`; selecting one of two duplicates removes one, not both. Pre-fix bug: legacy duplicates collapsed into one set element and got nuked together. Sacred regression guard.
* **Modals both carry `_blocks_undo: bool = True`** (attr AFTER docstring per sweep #6); ID-replace path in `LibraryPanel.add_entry` (same `record.id`) is NOT a collision — treated as "editing the same record" — and bypasses the modal flow. Tested by `tests/test_parts_bin_dedupe.py` (24 cases) + `tests/test_collision_flows.py` (5 cases).

## [INV-50] Adversarial sweep #10 (2026-05-20, pre-v1.0.0 follow-up to sweep #9)
11 HIGH fixes, `tests/test_sweep10.py` (25 cases). Targets bugs introduced by sweep #9 / `[INV-49]` + classes the earlier sweeps missed:
* **Batch-collision flow (`LibraryPanel.add_entries_batch`).** `_save_to_collection` pre-fix called `lib.add_entry(rec)` in a loop, each colliding rec stacking a `NameCollisionModal` while the post-loop toast claimed success before any modal resolved (and the modal stack soft cap would silently default-resolve at N=12). New batch API pre-classifies the whole batch, routes ONE collision flow, and fires `on_done(saved_names, replaced_names, cancelled)` once after the user resolves both modals. Toast emits from `on_done`, not from the loop. Diagnostics (stub-backbone warnings) also surface from `on_done` so they only fire for parts that actually landed.
* **Modal double-fire guard.** `ExactCopyConfirmModal` + `NameCollisionModal` carry a `_dismissed: bool` flag and a `_dismiss_once(payload)` helper. Pre-fix rapid double-clicks between the button-press handler and the async `pop_screen` completion could fire the same callback twice with potentially different payloads (Textual's `dismiss()` returns an AwaitComplete; the result-callback runs before the pop finishes). Every exit path (`_skip`/`_keep`/`_overwrite`/`_cancel_btn`/`action_cancel`) goes through `_dismiss_once`.
* **`_blocks_undo` retrofit.** 6 modals gained the flag: `EditSeqDialog`, `AddFeatureModal`, `FeatureEditModal`, `NewPlasmidModal`, `GrammarEditorModal`, `RestoreFromBackupModal`. Every one mutates canvas / record / persistent grammars / persistent library — app-level Ctrl+Z under them could race the in-progress edit (the bug class `[INV-41]` catches).
* **`RestoreFromBackupModal` cache-bust enumeration drift.** Pre-fix only reset 4 of 14 persisted-state caches. Sweep #9 added experiments / experiment projects / gels persisted files but the restore modal was never extended; restoring `experiments.json` from .bak left `_experiments_cache` stale and the next UI mutation silently overwrote the freshly-restored disk state. Now mirrors the full 13-cache enumeration from `_h_restore_pre_update_snapshot` (settings deliberately omitted — restoring settings would clobber the live app's pointer state mid-session).
* **Save-chain lock-release gap.** `_save_library` + `_save_parts_bin` pre-fix released `_cache_lock` BEFORE calling their mirror helper (`_sync_active_collection_plasmids` / `_sync_active_parts_bin_parts`), so Thread A's write + Thread B's write + Thread B's mirror + Thread A's stale-snapshot mirror could leave the mirror file out of sync with the live file. Mirror calls moved INSIDE the `with _cache_lock:` block. RLock allows re-entry from the mirror's own `_save_collections` / `_save_parts_bin_collections` chain. `_commit_library_entries` (post-modal commit path) similarly wraps its cache reseat in the lock.
* **Entry-vector RMW under lock.** `_set_entry_vector` + `_clear_entry_vectors_for_grammar` did load-mutate-save without `_cache_lock`. Two concurrent calls both read pre-state, both wrote, last writer lost the other's binding. The auto-bind flow's per-role loop amplified the race (TOCTOU on every iteration's `_get_entry_vector`). Now the full RMW runs under `_cache_lock`; RLock allows the nested `_save_entry_vectors` re-entry.
* **`_history_node_to_dict` stack-overflow fix.** Pre-fix recursed through `node.parents`, blowing Python's default recursion limit (1000) on hostile `.dna` imports carrying deeply-nested `<HistoryTree>` (32 MB cap can yield 3M+ nodes). Sibling helpers `walk`, `_history_node_count`, `HistoryScreen.populate` were already iterative for this reason; `_history_node_to_dict` was overlooked. Now iterative DFS with explicit `_HISTORY_NODE_MAX_DEPTH = 500` + `_HISTORY_NODE_MAX_NODES = 100_000` caps. Truncation surfaces via `_truncated` markers in the output dict.
* **`_bulk_import_folder` symlink refusal.** Pre-fix `path.is_file()` + `path.stat()` both FOLLOW symlinks — a hostile `.gb` symlinked to `/dev/zero` (devices report `st_size=0`) passed the size cap and `load_genbank` then read until OOM. Symlinks to `~/.ssh/id_rsa` or similar would also expose private data to any subsequent agent fetch of the imported entry. Now uses `path.lstat()` + `stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode)` to refuse symlinks outright; the per-file check fires AGAIN inside the import loop (defence-in-depth against TOCTOU between the children-scan and the load).
* **`_get_rotated_state` cache key.** Pre-fix keyed on `(id(self._seq), id(self._feats), o)`. Python's small-string interning + allocator reuse means two distinct strings can share the same id after one is GC'd, surfacing a stale rotated cache after an in-place edit that happened to reuse the previous string's address. `_build_seq_inputs` (line 6564) had been fixed for this same reason; rotated-state was overlooked. Now uses `(hash(self._seq), id(self._feats), o)`.
* **`except (AttributeError, Exception)` collapse.** `_reset_app_state_after_master_delete` carried the misleading tuple form — `Exception` subsumes `AttributeError`, so the clause was just `except Exception`. The pitfall-#1 "no bare except" sweep would have flagged this. Narrowed to `except (TypeError, AttributeError)` for the `.clear()` site (read-only mapping defence) and `except AttributeError` for the simple attribute access.

## [INV-51] Adversarial sweep #11 (2026-05-20, deferred items from sweep #10)
6 areas, `tests/test_sweep11.py` (18 cases). Closes the architecture-larger items sweep #10 deferred:
* **Active-pointer flush-sync.** Every active-pointer flip that's followed by a sync file write now calls `_settings_flush_sync()` between them — mirrors the sweep #9 fix for project switch. Applied to 4 sites: `LibraryPanel._on_select` (collection switch), `LibraryPanel._request_collection_delete` (delete-promote), `PartsBinPickerModal._open` (parts-bin switch), `PartsBinPickerModal._delete` (parts-bin delete-promote). Pre-fix a power loss between the async daemon-flush and the sync file write left settings.json saying OLD while the dependent file held NEW; next mutation mirrored NEW into OLD, silently overwriting OLD's data.
* **Cross-group worker RMW under `_cache_lock`.** Every worker that load-modify-saves the plasmid library — Constructor `_persist_assembly`, Traditional `_save_product_to_library`, Gibson `_gibson_save_worker`, Domesticator `_domesticator_library_mirror_worker` — now wraps the read-modify-save in `_cache_lock`. Pre-fix `exclusive=True` only serialised within ONE `@work` group; two workers from DIFFERENT groups could both read the same pre-state, both insert a new entry, both save — the second writer silently dropped the first's entry. RLock allows re-entry from the inner `_save_library` lock. Constructor + Gibson + Traditional ALSO re-disambiguate the unique `name` / `id` inside the lock to defend against a racing worker that took the originally-unique slot.
* **Settings flush worker drain on Master Delete.** `_perform_master_delete` now BUSY-WAITS up to 2s under `_settings_flush_lock` for `_settings_flush_running == False` before proceeding with the file unlinks. Pre-fix the daemon worker only held the lock long enough to swap out the pending payload; it then released the lock and called `_safe_save_json` on its own — so a wipe that cleared `_settings_flush_pending` mid-write would find settings.json resurrected by the worker's atomic `os.replace` AFTER the wipe pass.
* **UI freeze on bulk-import auto-bind closed via `@work` + batched writes.** `_auto_bind_entry_vectors_from_entries` previously ran N × M (entries × grammars) GenBank parses AND N synchronous `_set_entry_vector` writes on the UI thread immediately after `_bulk_import_folder` returned, freezing the UI for several seconds on a 100-entry × 3-grammar import. Now (a) gb_text is parsed ONCE per entry (not N × M), (b) all auto-detected bindings accumulate into a single `_set_entry_vectors_batch` call (one `_save_entry_vectors` write instead of N), (c) the whole detection runs in `LibraryPanel._auto_bind_worker` (`@work(thread=True, exclusive=True, group="auto_bind_evs")`); the import-success toast fires immediately + a follow-up notify lands when the worker finishes.
* **`_find_library_entry_by_id` helper.** New helper that takes `_cache_lock`, walks `_library_cache` directly, and clones only the matched entry — O(N) walk with O(1) cloned bytes instead of `_load_library()`'s O(N) cloned bytes per single-entry lookup. Migrated 4 hot-path callsites: `LibraryPanel._refresh_active_row` (status badge refresh), `LibraryPanel._on_delete` (delete confirmation), `SequencingScreen._do_align` (target plasmid resolve), `PlasmidApp._action_discard_changes` (Esc-from-canvas reload). Pre-fix each click paid a full library deepcopy.
* **Agent API hardening.** `_h_create_collection` now does an ATOMIC name-taken re-check inside `_cache_lock` so a GUI user (or second agent client) creating a same-named collection during the multi-second `_bulk_import_folder` window surfaces 409 instead of silently clobbering. `_h_hmmscan` collapses its "not found" / "rejected" / "too large" responses to a single generic 400 + log line — pre-fix an unauthenticated local process could probe arbitrary filesystem paths via the error-message differential. `_build_commercialsaas_features_packet_from_record` strips ASCII control characters from `<V text=>` qualifier attributes before serialising; pre-fix hostile values containing `\x01` etc. produced `.dna` files that BioPython accepted on write but `_safe_xml_parse` choked on during round-trip, silently dropping the colour + label overrides.

## [INV-52] UI audit sweep #12 (2026-05-20, four-phase render/modal/reactive pass)
`tests/test_sweep12.py` (23 cases). Audit ran each phase independently then consolidated:
* **Phase 1 — `action_aspect_inc/dec` early-exit at limit.** Holding `,` (widen) at the 5.0 cap or `.` (heighten) at the 0.5 floor previously fired `self.notify(...)` AND assigned `self._aspect = round(...)` on every key tick — Toast widgets queued up at the screen edge and the reactive churn fired Textual watchers redundantly. Post-fix: `new = round(...)`, `if new == self._aspect: return`, then assign + notify. Same equality-check pattern as the tooltip-cache identical-write guard which already documented "Avoid spamming reactive churn with identical writes."
* **Phase 2 — `_blocks_undo` retrofit onto 12 input-bearing modals.** Modals carrying user-facing `Input` / `TextArea` widgets (or committing persistent mutations) must opt in to the `_undo_blocked_by_modal` guard so app-level Ctrl+Z falls through to the Input's built-in undo instead of mauling the canvas underneath. Added to: `MultiRecordFastaModal`, `CustomEnzymeListModal`, `PrimerEditModal`, `ColorPickerModal`, `PartEditModal`, `SpeciesPickerModal`, `CollectionsModal`, `CollectionNameModal`, `NewCollectionModal`, `NamePlasmidModal`, `RenamePlasmidModal`, `MinPrimerBindingModal`. Same after-docstring placement as sweep #10 (`[INV-41]`) — `TestBlocksUndoAttrPlacement` white-box checks that the attr lands AFTER both `"""` triples.
* **Phase 3 — reactive & widget hygiene (no source changes; documented baseline).** Audit found ONE `watch_*` method (`PlasmidMap.watch_origin_bp`), ZERO `self.query(...)` callsites (everything routes through `query_one`), debounced search via `_SearchInput._on_input_changed` + `Input.Submitted`-only repopulate. `TestReactiveHygieneInvariants` regression-locks these as soft caps (≤3 watch methods total; `query_one` outnumbers `query` by 20×) so a future feature can't drift the baseline silently.
* **What this sweep did NOT do.** The render-perf audit produced exactly ONE fixable finding (aspect notify spam); the Explore agent's HIGH-tagged "id() on rotated state" finding was a false positive — `_get_rotated_state` returns CACHED `_rotated_seq` / `_rotated_feats` whose identity IS stable across calls with the same `_rotated_cache_key`, so the outer `_view_cache_key` keying on `id(disp_seq)` works correctly. Documented here so a future reader doesn't re-investigate.

## [INV-53] Synthesis composer (2026-05-20)
Full-screen gene-synthesis workbench reachable via the **Synthesis** menubar entry (sits between Mutagenize and Parts — the natural workflow position upstream of Parts → Constructor). `SynthesisScreen` hosts the `SynthesisEditor` widget (linear horizontal-scroll DNA editor that renders via `_build_seq_text(seq, feats, line_width=len(seq)+1, ...)` — one chunk = one row group, no wrap, all per-base overlays from the SequencePanel renderer: feature stripes + restriction overlay + AA translation). Tested by `tests/test_synthesis.py` (34 cases). Key invariants:
* **Sacred — document-model save.** `_loaded_id` carries the library entry id under edit. `_commit_save` builds a SeqRecord with `topology="linear"`, `molecule_type="DNA"`, and pushes through `LibraryPanel.add_entry`; the id-match branch silently replaces the existing entry (the id-match branch's "editing-in-place" path, not the collision modal flow). Save As resets `_loaded_id = None`, prompts `NamePlasmidModal` for a fresh name, and re-targets. Rename updates `_loaded_name` only; the user still hits Save to persist.
